# Library-vocab Registry (maintained master list)

**Created:** 2026-05-29 (v2.4 dedicated Library-vocab consolidation session, post-v115 audit).
**Status:** AUTHORITATIVE. This is the **single source of truth** for Library-vocab observational items. Per routine v2.4, Library-vocab items are tracked HERE and nowhere else — per-wiki narratives may *note* an observation but must *file* it into this registry (into a cluster or as a tracked standalone). No more loose "NEW candidate X" minting in wiki narratives that never gets tracked.

**Why this file exists:** through v114 the Library-vocab "PROVISIONAL" layer was reported as "~108 items." The v2.4 consolidation established that **~108 was a phantom count** — the running *sum of per-wiki "NEW candidate" mentions*, most never tracked again, many near-duplicate or one-off. There was never a maintained list. This file replaces the phantom count with a real, maintainable registry: **8 CONFIRMED + 7 themed clusters + a short live-standalone list + a retired set.**

---

## B. Tracked clusters (7) — the new PROVISIONAL structure

Each cluster is ONE tracked entry. Members are individual observations grouped by shared structural theme. A cluster promotes toward CONFIRMED only if a coherent sub-claim reaches N=3 cross-author + cross-vertical (routine §2). New per-wiki observations file into the matching cluster rather than minting standalones.

**LV-C1 — Vendor-Direct Skill-Authoring & Distribution.** Members: **Vendor-Official-Agent-Tooling-for-Own-Product/Offering → PROMOTED to CONFIRMED Library-vocab #21 at the v151 audit (2026-06-04)** [N=3 v114 gsap library-vendor + v126 Every methodology/editorial-vendor + v149 Scrapling library-vendor; cross-author + cross-vertical + cross-scale all PASS; **promote-broad-class** chosen over split — the library-vendor (v114+v149) / methodology-editorial-vendor (v126) distinction is recorded as **internal sub-flavors inside #21**, NOT separate entries (anti-inflation: a split would create more entries, one a fresh N=1; #8's 8a/8b precedent). **This member is now promoted-out of the cluster — see §A #21.**], Vendor-Skill-Embeds-Recommendation-Bias (v114), Multi-Registry-Distribution (v89), Canonical-Snippet-Auto-Insertion (v88), Clone-as-Sibling-Directory (v88), Zero-Dependency-Bridge-Tool (v88), Progressive-Tiered-Skill-Authoring-Methodology (v121 CodexKit; 5-layer Intent/Knowledge/Execution/4C-Verification/Evolution + 3-tier T1→T2→T3 structure — filed as an ADJACENT member: third-party author, NOT vendor-direct, so it does not inflate the (a)-7-sister framing), **Commercial-AI-Startup OSS Domain-Skill-Library as Brand/Community-Infrastructure + Companion-App Funnel (v124 K-Dense scientific-agent-skills — ADJACENT member: distinct from v114 product-vendor-for-own-product because the skills are general-domain scientific skills, and the company monetizes via a *separate* hosted platform + a *free OSS* BYOK desktop app; the OSS library is brand/community-infrastructure + top-of-funnel, not a product-usage manual)**. *Theme:* how skills/tools are authored + pushed into agents. (a)-7-sister framing for the v114 vendor-direct item lives here. Cluster status: live, N=watch.

**LV-C2 — Cost / Capacity Economics of Free-or-Cheap Inference.** Members: Free-Tier-Stacking-as-Capacity-Aggregation (v112), Cost-Attribution-via-LiteLLM (v89), Cost-Attribution-via-External-Pricing-Service (v109), Quota-Arbitrage-Across-Vendor-Internal-Surfaces (v67), Sticky-Session-Model-Affinity (v112), **API-Relay/Middle-Layer-Reseller-Economy (v117 CodexPlusPlus; JOJO Code/AIGoCode/PackyCode sponsor a config-switcher that points a coding agent at relay endpoints — filed at v120 audit)**. *Promotable sub-claim:* **"Cost-Attribution-via-External-Service" N=2** (LiteLLM v89 + pricing-service v109) → PROMOTION-ELIGIBLE at N=3 (NOT advanced by v117 — relay-reseller-economy is a distinct sub-theme, not cost-attribution). (#13 OSS-with-hosted-Pro-SaaS-tier-on-MIT-base = RETIRED v96; historical member.)

**LV-C3 — Doc-Coherence: Anti-Drift vs Drift-Exhibited.** Members: "names-the-drift-and-drifts-anyway" inverse-pole **N=2** (v66 agentmemory + v113 ai-engineering-from-scratch; sub-observation of Pattern #81), **Self-Referential-Methodology-Demonstration N=3 (v87 + v126 compound-engineering-plugin [in-repo `docs/brainstorms/` + `docs/plans/` dogfooding its Brainstorm→Plan→Compound loop] + v131 harness [`_workspace/` auditor/content/scout/strategist team that audited+planned harness's OWN launch, following harness's own `{phase}_{agent}_{artifact}` output convention]; promotion-eligible at next audit ~v134-v135. ⚠️ v131 is a SOFT 3rd instance — the `_workspace/` artifacts are NOT explicitly stamped "generated by harness"; structural evidence only [naming-convention match to SKILL.md Phase 5-1 + harness-as-its-own-subject]. Audit may keep N=3 or downgrade to N=2-plus-adjacent.)**, Marketing-Site-vs-README-Divergence (v80), **skill-count-drift-with-spec/security-CI-but-no-count-CI (v124 scientific-agent-skills — 141↔142 across opening/What's-Included/badge; WEAK member, does NOT advance the N=2 inverse-pole to N=3 — a minor count-inconsistency, not a load-bearing manifest drift)**. *Cross-ref:* Pattern #81-proper (Manifest-Drift-as-First-Class-CI-Concern, v64 claude-seo) is the opposite pole and lives at the Pattern layer. Cluster status: live; the N=2 inverse-pole is the strongest member (N=3 watch).

**LV-C4 — Cadence / Velocity micro-signals.** Members: High-Development-Cadence-relative-to-Star-Velocity **N=2** (v89+v107), High-Release-Cadence-at-Micro-Scale (v89), Release-Cadence-Discipline (v88), High-Fork-Ratio-at-Micro-Scale-with-Sustained-Dev (v121 CodexKit; 0.526 fork-ratio at 19★ + 8 releases — explicitly distinct from CONFIRMED #17 Try-Once-and-Move-On, which lacks sustained dev; here the high fork-ratio coexists with active maintenance). *Cross-ref:* Pattern #52 (velocity sub-classes) + #16/#17 (CONFIRMED) cover the headline engagement/velocity vocabulary; this cluster holds the residual cadence one-offs. Status: live, N=watch.

**LV-C5 — Design-Skill Composition Vocabulary.** Members: Compositional-Mix-&-Match (v87), Universal-Modifier-Composable (v87), Multi-Attribute-Style-Schema (v87), Visual-Directions-Taxonomy **N=2** (v81+v83), CO-EXISTENT-framing-axes **N=2** (v82+v83). *Theme:* design-skill-local composition vocabulary. Largely vertical-bound (design-skill cluster); unlikely to cross-promote. Status: live but vertical-capped.

**LV-C6 — Session / Prompt-Persistence & Autonomy.** Members: Tri-Layer-Prompt-Persistence (v88), Local-Conversation-Plus-Bridge-Summaries (v88), Auto-Execution-Mode-Prerequisite (v88), Autonomy-vs-Session-Handoff-Continuum (v79). *Theme:* teleport/harness-local session+autonomy vocabulary. Status: live, N=watch.

**LV-C7 — Packaging / Runtime-Substrate one-offs.** Members: Single-Tool-No-Fallback-Packaging (v79), Rust-for-Observability-Perf (v89), Bun-primary (v84, OVERRIDE-caveat), **Tauri-Desktop Management-GUI for a Coding Agent N=3 (v73 cc-switch + v117 CodexPlusPlus + v153 ai-switcher — Tauri desktop control-planes that manage *another* coding agent's config/providers/accounts; v73+v117 filed at v120 audit, v153 ai-switcher filed at ship 2026-06-04 = the genuine 3rd management-GUI [native-macOS account/quota control-plane for Claude Code + Codex + Antigravity])**. *v120 audit note (honest downgrade):* the ship-time "LV-C7 Tauri-desktop-harness → N=3 (incl. v118 OpenHuman)" claim is **CORRECTED to N=2** — v118 OpenHuman is a Tauri desktop app but a **standalone agent assistant**, NOT a management-GUI-for-another-agent, so it is **adjacent, not a clean 3rd instance**; the "archetype-vs-Tauri-stack-popularity" check (flagged at v117/v118) resolves toward *don't promote* — "uses Tauri" is incidental; the tight claim is the management-GUI-for-another-agent shape (v73+v117 only). *Cross-ref:* Pattern #84 84d Hardware-Substrate-Tolerance (CONFIRMED N=4) covers the structural substrate-tolerance claim. Status: live (the non-management-GUI one-offs remain in this cluster); **the management-GUI sub-claim was PROMOTED-OUT to CONFIRMED Library-vocab #22 at the v158 audit (2026-06-04→05)** [N=3 v73 cc-switch + v117 CodexPlusPlus + v153 ai-switcher; broad-class promotion, sub-flavors recorded inside #22; see §A]. **This member is now promoted-out of the cluster — see §A #22.**

| **Text-Space Skill Optimizer — Training-Loop Optimization of a Frozen Agent's Natural-Language Skill Document via Validation-Gated Edits** | **N=1** | **v178 SkillOpt (`microsoft` / Microsoft Research)** | **filed v178; CORPUS-FIRST — a tool/methodology that treats an agent's natural-language SKILL DOCUMENT as the *trainable state* of a frozen model and improves it with a deep-learning-style training loop in TEXT space (no weight updates): a separate optimizer model turns scored rollouts into bounded add/delete/replace edits, gated by a textual learning-rate budget, and a candidate edit is ACCEPTED ONLY IF it strictly improves a held-out validation score (loop = rollout→reflect→edit→validate; rejected-edit buffer; epoch-wise slow/meta update; deployable best_skill.md ≈300–2,000 tok; zero inference-time overhead). ## E. Standing anti-re-accumulation rules (routine v2.4)

1. **Single registry.** This file is the only tracked location. Wiki narratives may NOTE an observation but must FILE it here (cluster or standalone) or omit it.
2. **Registration cap.** A wiki may register at most **2 genuinely-NEW standalone** Library-vocab candidates; further observations must join an existing cluster or be omitted. (Formalizes the v115 freeze.)
3. **Auto-retire (time-aware — AMENDED v151 audit 2026-06-04, operator sign-off).** A standalone N=1 is auto-retired at the next audit only once it has gone **both ≥15 wikis AND ≥30 days** without strengthening ("15 wikis OR 30 days, whichever is longer" — retire on the threshold satisfied later). Mechanically checked against this file. The 30-day floor protects recent items during burst-shipping; the 15-wiki ceiling still retires genuinely-stale items under slow cadence. Re-registerable on a genuine 2nd instance. (Routine: `v2.4 §28.3` + `v2.6 §39`.)
4. **Count discipline.** Report the **tracked-registry count** (8 CONFIRMED + 7 clusters + N live standalones), NOT the cumulative-mention sum. The "~108" figure is officially deprecated as a non-maintenance artifact.
5. **Filing is an ACT, not a claim (NEW, v120 audit).** "File it into the registry" means **edit this file in the same session as the wiki ship** — not write "filed into LV-Cx" in the wiki narrative and move on. **v120 audit FINDING:** v116–v119 narratives *claimed* to file observations (LV-C2 relay-economy, LV-C7 Tauri-desktop) but the registry was **never actually edited** — the exact "note that never gets tracked" failure mode §28 exists to prevent, reproduced one level up. The cap (rule 2) and count-discipline (rule 4) held; the filing step did not. Remediated at the v120 audit (the v116–v119 observations are now actually filed above). Going forward: no wiki ship is "done" until its registry observations are written into this file.

**NO MINT — the §C-mint alternative** *"Harness-Level Inference-Time Trajectory Conditioning — a host-agent preset that constrains the first request's API-visible tool schema and blanks every auto-injected context to select the model's reasoning trajectory, then auto-promotes to a resident catalog on a durable session event, with the intervention's dose-response measured"* — is **RECORDED + DECLINED.** It is genuinely distinct on mechanism and **corpus-first by grep** (`defer_loading`/`Tool Search`/`progressive tool`/`tool gating`/`lazy schema`/`attractor`/`resident catalog`/`dose-response`/`first-request` all **0** across `_state/03c` + this file + `_patterns/02b`), and was hand-checked distinct from every near neighbour: **v140 google_workspace_mcp §C** (the closest row — **NOT its N=2**: that row is scoped by its own text to *"a 2nd **MCP server** with graduated/tiered tool exposure"* [v238 is a harness preset, not an MCP server] and its rationale is explicitly **API-quota + permission-scope** applied as a **deployment-time static tier flag**, whereas v238 is a **per-session runtime phase machine keyed on durable events, for reasoning quality** — same silhouette, different animal) · **v144 headroom §C** (pre-LLM interceptor that **compresses** for token economy; that row already distinguishes v140's tool-registration gating) · **v223 openinterpreter §C** (harness **emulation** — swaps whole harness personalities; v238 is one measured lever inside one harness) · **v178 SkillOpt §C** (optimises a skill **document** in text space via a validation gate — different object) · **v168 ponytail §C** (a hand-authored behaviour ruleset **injected** as prose; v238 **removes**, and its lever is the API tool schema) · **v186 DeepSpec** (speculative decoding — weights/decoding layer, not the request surface). **DECLINED on five grounds: (1) emphatically NOT world-first — Anthropic ships the mechanism first-party** (the **Tool Search Tool with `defer_loading: true` STRIPS deferred tools from the initial system prompt** and restores them on demand via a search tool, documented **77K → 8.7K tokens** on request #1 = the same bootstrap→discovery→promote shape), **and the literature is thick** (arXiv:2604.21816 dynamic tool gating + lazy schema loading, Apr 2026 — explicitly tying tool-visibility minimisation to preserved reasoning quality; arXiv:2605.24660 *"How Many Tools Should an LLM Agent See?"*; arXiv:2512.15274 *"Well Begun, Half Done"*; arXiv:2506.22058 *"Lost at the Beginning of Reasoning"*; plus a least-privilege tool-restriction literature framed as security); **(2) the distinctive contribution is a RATIONALE + a MEASUREMENT, not a capability** — every documented staged-tool system justifies itself with token economy or least privilege, this one with *reasoning-trajectory selection*, and §C vocab is **agent-capability-shaped** → the **PixelRAG v211 discipline** (*corpus-first for a technique ≠ a mintable §C class*, especially when not world-first) applies exactly; **(3) §28** draw-the-circle; **(4) the benefit is unestablished and the two replications CONFLICT** — minting would encode a contested claim as vocabulary; **(5) a weak, now-DEAD anchor in an already-crowded class** (v0.1.0, maintenance-only from 2026-08-17, and the class is **not anchored here**: `yjh051108/dsh-routing-suite` ~5.7k★ different author does *"first-turn tool injection"* + a dual-attractor policy paper, `Tiger3807861189/J-Space-Cognition-Suite-V3.6` does model-agnostic inference-time cognitive control as a Skill with effects reported across Claude, and the subject's own issue **#53** is *"Convergent two-phase design from an independent lineage"*).** → a corpus-knowledge data-point + a **DEFERRED watch axis** *"harness-level inference-time trajectory conditioning — manipulating the first-request tool surface (and blanking auto-injections) to select a model's reasoning trajectory, dose-response measured."* ⚠️ **FIFTH consecutive NO-MINT in the DSH chain but on DIFFERENT grounds than v236/v237** (those were presentation-not-capability; this is not-world-first + rationale-not-mechanism) — recorded so the audit does not read reflexive declines.

**⚠️ AUDIT ACTION FLAGGED (NOT self-executed):** the **v140 §C row "Graduated / Least-Privilege Tool-Exposure for a Large-Surface MCP Server"** carries *"5-wiki stale-watch ~v155"* and is **still N=1 at v238 (~98 wikis)**, past both §28.3/§39 floors. The audit should **RETIRE or GENERALISE** it — generalising to cover **staged tool exposure regardless of rationale** would make v238 its **N=2** and resolve this ship's boundary question in one move. A promotion/retirement is an audit act (the v232/v235/v237 rule).

**EVIDENCE STATUS (load-bearing, recorded because any future use must carry it):** **ESTABLISHED** — the preset reliably changes the *style* of the model's first reasoning chain (#65 @TipsyDrifter, 3×3 randomised block: **9/9 separated perfectly by preset**, *"real and very robust"*); and auto-injected context can **destroy** the effect (#6 @slicenferqin: **~9 KB** skill catalog → **0/9** vs ~81% without, which explains why third parties with local skills *"could never replicate"*). **NOT ESTABLISHED** — that it makes the model *better*: #65 ability **+3.3, 95% CI [−2.6, +9.3]**, ANOVA F(2,6)=1.05, **not significant**. **DISPUTED** — #51 (@JimMilk, 11 rounds × 3 OSes): Ability **85–90, 98/99 NOT reproduced**, trajectory showed **no correlation with scores**, *"not reproducible on current weights"*, losses traced to a **model-layer defect** (F3-05 session_id implicit authorization) *"unrelated to OS/preset/trajectory."* **RETRACTED** — #60 (@MolecularFullerene, a **third party**) showed the headline scores had been **retroactively re-attributed** (those runs used `pwsh`+`read` → the full 25-tool catalog; the exact Minimal pair came later, commit `a1e1c1d`); the author accepted it and the README carries the caveat, **but the repo TAGLINE still advertises "Project2 98/99."** **The two independent replications disagree with each other.**

the author documented the caveat, added that the generic prefab *"must not"* carry those scores, and links both failing replications — while the tagline still advertises the retracted number) → a possible new 83 sub-mechanism, **for the audit to decide**. · **⭐ LV-C2 cost-economics — a research project KILLED BY INFERENCE PRICING**: eval rounds went **~¥2 → ~¥12 (~$0.28 → ~$1.70)** and `FAREWELL.md` (2026-08-17) states 「继续在没有验证能力的情况下堆功能，是对用户不负责任」 (*"continuing to pile on features without verification capability is irresponsible to users"*) → the citable lesson: **for inference-time research the binding constraint is the cost of EVALUATION, not development** (a tiny absolute number × many required rounds = project death). · **#20 Token-Economy-Quantification QUALIFIED-ADJACENT, NO N-bump** — it quantifies the intervention's *behavioural* effect (we/let-me rates, hit rates, ablations), not artifact token footprint → **N stays 4** (the ponytail v168 handling). · **#57 recorded, N NOT incremented** — the **FOURTH consecutive ship in one dependency graph** and a **third structural kind of DSH extension** (v235 host → v236 UI-seam replacement → v237 UI-layer plugin-as-platform → **v238 agent preset / row composition**); Per the clean-2nd-instance precedent (camofox v179 / codebase-memory-mcp v172 / Strix v190 / OmniRoute v208) this is **recorded, not self-promoted — a promotion is an audit act (the v232 rule).** ⚠️ **The audit should weigh a mechanical difference between the two instances:** geti symlinked a **skill suite**; ToolJet symlinks a skill directory **and the root context file**, and the root-context-file half is the load-bearing one (it is what makes drift between `CLAUDE.md` and `AGENTS.md` *impossible* rather than merely discouraged). ⚠️ Also note the instance is **one day old on `main`** (authored 2026-07-28, squash-merged 2026-08-18) — durability is unproven.

**Other axes touched, no N self-incremented.** **#83** honest-deficiency disclosure ×2 — `.env.example` documents `TOOLJET_WORKFLOW_SANDBOX_BYPASS` *with its own warning* (*"Python code will run without isolation when bypassed"*), and `AGENTS.md` discloses that the pre-commit hook lints frontend files only. **#66** supply-chain **positive exemplar** — **exactly 1 lifecycle script across 103 tracked `package.json` files** (the root's `husky install`; zero `postinstall`/`preinstall`) and **zero lockfile mirrors**, joining **pi v228** and the v241/v242 pair. **v184 (Osmani) / v189 (loop-engineering) grill→PRD→AFK→QA pipeline** — `plans/widget-css-class.md` carries `(Grill correction — …)`, `(PM correction, 2026-06-18)` and `Type: AFK`: **the corpus's first sighting of that pipeline's artifacts committed inside a production repository**, recorded as an **independent convergence, NOT a #57 dependency**. ⚠️ **NO #57 claim** — the `.gitignore` names `docs/superpowers/` and `.codegraph/`, but `.codegraph/` was **not verified** to be corpus subject v70 and a directory name is not evidence.

**⚠️ A prior-art claim was DISCARDED as confabulation:** a research agent asserted that *"the AGENTS.md format was originally developed by Anthropic … definitely before ToolJet (2021)."* AGENTS.md did not exist in 2021, and the agent conflated it with Anthropic's Agent Skills (`SKILL.md`) format. **No origin attribution is made on that axis**; the only prior-art fact carried forward is the vault's own verified corpus record (v213). The same agent also confabulated that ToolJet's commits omit the model version (**899 of 905 trailer lines name one**) and that no `Claude-Session:` trailer exists (**11 do**) — three confabulations in one output, all about commits it never read from the clone.

**TWO NEW DECISION RULES (governance, not vocabulary).** **D26** — in a squash-merge repository a grep over commit **bodies** counts **pre-squash** commits, not commits: ToolJet squashes 35.6% of its history (6,087/17,101 subjects end `(#NNNN)`), and 165 commits carry 905 trailer lines (×5.48, max 143 in one); squashing also **hides commits entirely** — the agent layer's original `75eef168d` (2026-07-28) is not an ancestor of `main`. **D27** — declare the **ref population** of every git count and re-check it before accepting a refutation: `HEAD` and `--all` differ here by 8,512 commits (33%) and 56% on trailers, and **this ship's adversarial verifier returned REFUTED twice, on two unrelated claims, both times by silently switching to `--all`** while both claims were correct as stated. **D27 extends v241's D21** — a verifier can check the *right* claim against the *wrong* population and return a false refutation.

**Execution:** 16-agent `Workflow` (6 map surfaces → 8 adversarial verifiers → prior-art → completeness critic), **16/16 completed, 0 errors, ~1.69M subagent tokens, 430 tool uses, 778s** — `Workflow` **stable across five consecutive ships** (v239→v243). **12 errors caught, 4 of them the analyst's**, and 3 of those 4 caught by hand rather than by asking an agent.

**THREE NEW DECISION RULES (governance, not vocabulary).** **⭐⭐ D31 — a SCOPE VERDICT is a claim about a DATE, not a property of a subject.** v23 filed this repo OUTSIDE-SCOPE (*"not agent infrastructure — training-only … Direct adoption: NONE"*) and was right on the day; everything that makes it in-scope landed 63–93 days later (#6547 Local Agent Guides CI 06-22 → #6613 `unsloth start` 07-03 → **#7326 local subagents for Claude Code/Codex/OpenCode/Pi 07-22** → #7329 Claude plan mode 07-24). **OUTSIDE-SCOPE is the one verdict that CLOSES the file, therefore the one most likely to rot unobserved — every prior outside-scope subject is un-audited by construction.** Detector = the **description field** + the **top-level directory shares**. ⭐ **Enumerable work-list: v8 build-your-own-x · v20 fish-speech · v21 system-prompts-leaks · v22 LlamaFactory · v23 Unsloth (crossed). Ask how the token travels before weighting the CORS finding.**

**§F v246 needle (2026-08-19) — NO-MINT ship, 0 §C changes; recorded in the shim + `_state/03c`.** `cactus-compute/needle` = **"Needle 2"**, a **45M-parameter foundation model for tool calling, device use and structured extraction** shipped as a single **14MB binary** running in ~**28MB RAM**; Apache-2.0; the repo is the **Python package** (inference bindings + a JAX reference implementation + LoRA fine-tuning + a quantizing exporter), **44 files / 10,824 lines**, source-cloned twice. **GOAL-ALIGNED INCLUDE 3/4** [(a) FAIL — Cactus Compute, Inc., corporate-not-Anthropic per §41 · **(b) MODERATE keys the tier** per §40 on operator direction, the OFF-GOAL reading recorded as the defensible alternative · (c) STRONG ⚠️ tempered · (d) STRONG].

**The §C standalone candidate — *"Sub-100M On-Device Tool-Calling Foundation Model Shipped as an Agent-Callable Library with Constrained Decoding"* — is DECLINED on five independent grounds, three of them this registry's own written discipline:**

1. **The MODEL-TIER RULE binds, and this is its 5th data point.** §F v176 GLM-5 verbatim: *"**NO §C standalone minted** — a single goal-adjacent frontier model is not a recurring capability class; **§C vocab is tool/capability-shaped**"*; §F v193 TimesFM **DECLINED** a *"corpus-first time-series-forecasting FM"* mint on identical reasoning despite being the corpus's first forecasting FM. ⇒ **the descriptive Model/Inference-Substrate tier becomes N=5** — GLM-5 v176 · DeepSpec v186 · TimesFM v193 · PixelRAG v211 · **needle v246** (**bookkeeping only; counts unchanged**).
2. **NOT world-first, in a crowded band.** FunctionGemma 270M · LFM2 / LFM2.5 · Apple FM on-device · Octopus v2 · the Gorilla / ToolLLM / xLAM / Hammer line — the README names three of them as peers. Grammar-constrained decoding is **standard practice** (llama.cpp GBNF, Outlines, XGrammar, Guidance, JSON mode). **What is distinctive is SCALE — and scale is a number, not a class** (~5–6× below the smallest widely-cited peer) ⇒ the **PixelRAG v211** discipline: *corpus-first-for-a-technique ≠ a mintable §C class.* ⚠️ **An agent's "WORLD-FIRST at 45M" verdict was REJECTED** — an absence-of-evidence argument, **falsified inside the same product line** (the org's own `cactus` repo documents Needle at **26m**), and it confuses a number with a class.
3. **domain-not-capability** — phones / wearables / smart home / robots (the **meetily v196** + **AIRI v210** discipline).
4. **§28 anti-inflation** — no cluster to join, no N=2 partner in the corpus.
5. ⭐ **The distinctive machinery is NOT IN THE SUBJECT.** `needle/__init__.py:38-48` binds exactly **four** C symbols, so the **learned tool-retrieval head** (the "top-5" is not in this repo), the **byte-level grammar** and inference-time **confidence** all live in a binary fetched at runtime from a Hugging Face model repo. Minting here would mean minting a class on **prose describing unread code** ⇒ **v242's D25 applied to a COMPONENT rather than a subject.** There is **no benchmark data, no eval harness and no reproduction script** anywhere in the repo.

**⇒ Counts 46 top-level / 11 CONFIRMED Library-vocab UNCHANGED. §C live standalones 51 unchanged. Surface ≈58 unchanged.**

**Instance-strengthening RECORDED, not self-executed (a promotion is an audit act — the v232 rule):**

**⭐⭐ THE CORPUS CONTRIBUTION — the tool-catalogue thread reaches the MODEL layer and spans two layers for the first time.** Every prior treatment was harness-level: **v167 audit** (the vault's own ~54K tool-catalogue floor) · **v238 dsh-anchored-standard** (blank the auto-injections; Anthropic ships it first-party as `defer_loading`) · **v245 Unsloth** (**measured** it — *"~18k is System tools"* — and named `--tools`). **Needle 2 is the first subject to move the problem into the WEIGHTS:** a learned head renders only the **top-5 of a large declared catalogue per turn** and constrains the grammar to that subset, **so the catalogue never has to fit in the prompt at all.** ⇒ **N=4.** ⚠️ With the caveat from ground 5: the head is in the binary and nothing in this repo measures whether it works.

### §F — v247 ego lite (`citrolabs/ego-lite`) — 2026-08-19 — **NO MINT**

**Subject.** "ego lite", a Chromium-based **macOS browser built so a human and any number of external AI agents share ONE browser** (per-agent isolated **Task Spaces** inheriting the user's login state), plus **`ego-browser`**, the open connection layer exposing the browser to any agent CLI as an in-page JavaScript tool surface. **MIT repo; the browser itself is a closed-source `.dmg`.** Page-stated **11.9k★ / 624 forks** (§37.4 — mocked API, **NOT #52**). Org **CitroLabs**; dominant committer pseudonymous ("Tachikoma" / `section9lab@`). ✅ **SOURCE-CLONED TWICE** (HEAD `c46a439e`, 2026-08-10; **150 tracked files / ~26,800 lines**; **245 commits `HEAD` / 415 `--all`**, 70 refs, 21 tags, **2 roots** — `f4ae5dcf` is HEAD's ancestor, `144b0734` is an orphan). **GOAL-ALIGNED INCLUDE 3/4** [(a) FAIL §41 · (b) **STRONG** — cleanly on the goal-#1 agent-capability substrate, no §40 · (c) STRONG ⚠️ tempered · (d) STRONG]. **COLLISION CLEAN.** **Pattern #57: NONE.**

**MINT — NO, on four independent grounds** (the collision agent and the critic concurred independently):
1. **Form-factor-within-a-genre.** A browser is a product genre — ruled twice already: **lobehub v222** (*fame is not a mint*; world-canonical ≠ world-first) and **hermes-webui v227**.
2. **A combination is not a capability class.** All three components are occupied by strong prior art: *daily-use browser whose agent reaches your logins* → **Claude in Chrome / ChatGPT Atlas / Perplexity Comet**; *isolated parallel workspaces in one browser* → **Arc Spaces** (the name is literally Arc's) / **Firefox Multi-Account Containers** / **Chrome profiles** / the whole **anti-detect** category (**CloakBrowser v69**, **camofox v179**); *external-agent-drivable browser* → **CDP/Playwright / Playwright MCP / Chrome DevTools MCP / browser-use v41 / Vercel agent-browser**. §C vocabulary is tool/capability-shaped. ⚠️ **Arc and Brave already ship first-class agent APIs, Arc's with a declarative JSON permission system** — a control this subject lacks.
3. **The distinctive machinery is NOT IN THE SUBJECT** — the *"kernel-level customization / strongest Snapshot / deeply nested iframes"* differentiator lives in the closed binary (`driver/observe.ts` ≈139 lines calling `browserEgo().snapshot()`). The same ground on which **v246 needle** declined.
4. **§28** anti-re-accumulation + the N=1 gate.

⭐ **RECORDED NOT EXECUTED — the losing alternative (DEFERRED watch axis, N=1):** **"Human+Agent Shared-State Co-Habitation with Per-Workspace Ownership Handoff."** The genuinely distinctive artifact is not the browser but **the per-Space ownership state machine** — `agent | agentDelegatedToUser | user`, with `handOff` / `takeOver` / `claim` / `waitForAgentControl` as first-class operations, a tabulated per-helper policy for user-owned Spaces, and a doctrine naming deference as success. Repeatable beyond browsers (an IDE, a terminal multiplexer, a shared design canvas) ⇒ a real N=2 candidate if a second implementation ships. ⚠️ Not minted: **an absence of evidence is not evidence of firstness** (the argument a v246 agent got wrong and had rejected), and **a promotion is an audit act** (the v232 rule).

**Instance-strengthening — recorded, NOT self-incremented (N-tallies are audit bookkeeping):**
- **Library-vocab #12** (agent-facing / LLM-routing artifacts, **CONFIRMED N=5+**) — via `skills/ego-browser/SKILL.md`, which on the merits is **the strongest agent-facing contract in the corpus** (it governs *behaviour*, not just calls: a **write-probe** discipline, a tabulated ownership model, and *"Honoring it **is** the correct outcome here; pushing the goal forward anyway is the failure"*).
- **Cross-harness packaging family** — **four harness targets from one canonical skill**: `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` (against `anthropic.com/claude-code/marketplace.schema.json`) + `.codex-plugin/plugin.json` + `skills/ego-browser/agents/openai.yaml` + **two real git symlinks** (mode 120000) at `.claude/skills/ego-browser` and `.codex/skills/ego-browser`, both → `../../skills/ego-browser`, both resolving.
- ⚠️ **The symlink-vs-stub class recurs at a THIRD independent organisation** (v243 ToolJet → v244 OpenSandbox → v247): real symlinks for the two harness skill dirs, while **root `install.md` is a 41-byte REGULAR FILE (mode 100644)** whose whole content is the literal string `skills/ego-browser/references/install.md` — a hand-written pseudo-symlink in a repo that demonstrably knows how to make a real one. **v244's exact error** (the drift-proof mechanism in the weaker place). Recorded; the mechanism's N-tally and any promotion remain an audit act.

**⚠️ NOT a palmier-pro v192 instance — boundary defended.** That standalone (**N=5, promotion-flagged**) requires a **first-party MCP server** *and* a **retrofit**. ego lite ships **a skill, not an MCP server** (verified: no MCP server anywhere in the tree) and was **built agent-first from the start**. **Both halves of the row's name fail ⇒ do NOT count v247 toward it.** Getting this wrong would silently fire that row's promotion trigger.

**⭐ TWO NEW RULES (the ship's transferable output):**
- **D34 — a committed CI rule is not an enforced one.** (**v244 D28 in reverse:** D28 said an absent config file is not an absent check; the converse holds.) Anchor: `main-pr-source.yml` fails any PR into `main` not sourced from `dev`; added 2026-06-01; **since then 82 PR merges into `main`, 16 from `dev`, 66 from branches the gate rejects — including HEAD**, while `CONTRIBUTING.md:283` tells contributors to *"branch from latest `main`"*, `:322` says merge via `dev`, the pre-commit hook gates on `origin/dev`, and `main`/`dev` have **diverged** (32 ahead / 20 behind). ⚠️ Whether the gate is a *required* status check lives in branch-protection settings, **not in the tree** — so the claim is a measurement, never an accusation. ⭐ **DETECTOR (portable, one command — this ship's method contribution alongside v246's `grep -rni "silent" .`): take a gate's own predicate and test it against the history it was supposed to govern.** *A gate you have never seen fail is not a gate you have seen pass.*
- **D35 — prose goes stale in proportion to how little MACHINERY and how few READERS touch it, not by whether its audience is a human or a machine.** (**Refines D22**, which predicted that *agent-facing* prose goes stale first.) Anchor: a **within-repo controlled comparison** — same repo, same authors, same 73 days. The shipped `SKILL.md` (209 lines, read on every agent run, rewritten by CI, published to two registries) is **clean**; the developer-facing docs have **five verified defects**: a link to a `SKILL.zh.md` deleted at `1a7f293`; the Four Principles attributed to an `AGENTS.md` that contains none of them; `@eN` taught in four places when `AGENTS.md:17` explicitly warns *"`@21`, **not** `@e21`"*; `AGENTS.md` naming a `run-e2e.sh` that no longer exists; and `AGENTS.md` describing `npm run e2e` as a suite `package.json` does not map it to. ⚠️ **Vault application is exact:** `CLAUDE.md` is high-contact and maintained; the `_state/03c-**v183**` filename label and the shim↔`_patterns/06` divergence are the lowest-contact surfaces — and **the v246 E3 error (a stale shim premise fed to 14 agents) is this same mechanism.**

**⭐ AND THE HEADLINE, for the record:** the repository **declares the closed-source boundary unprompted** — `README.md` (License) *"The ego lite browser is a separate, free download"* and `AGENTS.md:3-5` *"provided by the **closed-source** ego lite app … **not** the browser itself."* ⇒ **against v244 → v245 → v246 this CLOSES A SET OF FOUR** in which the licence in prose did not describe the artifact that runs (over-assert → under-assert → split-and-silent → **split-and-declared**). It is the counter-example the set needed, and it costs one sentence. ⚠️ **The other omission tested and DROPPED:** the unmentioned paid `ego` / Business tier is **not** a v246-shaped omission — `lite.ego.app/enterprise` prices the free tier *"$0 forever"* and sells **support/SLA/onboarding, not withheld features**; nothing about the user's rights or software is affected, so the README's *"Free ✓"* row is defensible.

**⭐ THE THESIS (why the ship is worth its space): this project writes excellent checks and does not connect them.** Its own doctrine is *"write the check first"* (`CONTRIBUTING.md` §12, Goal-Driven Execution). It then hand-rolls **real mutation testing** on `acorn` aimed exactly at the predicates whose silent failure does damage (`isAgentOwned`, `findMatchingTaskSpace`, `boxModelCenter`) — **unwired**; writes **a style linter for the prose that instructs an LLM** (prose-vs-**doctrine**, not v240's prose-vs-code) — **unwired**; and builds a skill-publish pipeline (version normalisation, **a token-leak scanner over the release archive**, a SHA256-pinned CLI, dry-run, post-publish verify) that **triggers on a tag pattern no tag in the repo matches — it has never fired**. The one check wired in all four places validates the site-learning **data format**. ⭐ **Conversely it gets right the thing v246 got wrong:** its real-browser suite (24 modules / 31 cases) cannot run on `ubuntu-latest`, so instead of letting it **skip** in the release gate (v246: *pytest reports a skip as success*) it moved it into the **pre-commit hook, where the closed browser exists**, and CI claims only what it ran ⇒ **PORTABLE RULE: put the test where the dependency is, and let the gate claim only what it ran.** ⚠️ Bypassable with `--no-verify`, opt-in, and undisclosed in `CONTRIBUTING.md` = a silent contract.

🔴 **PILOT: DO NOT INSTALL.** *The only configuration in which it is safe for this operator is the one that deletes the reason to use it* — its feature is inheriting real logins; decline that and it is a closed binary from an unidentified team driving pages that don't matter, which Playwright does in the open on three platforms. Candidate sourcing fails on the **RATIFIED ADR** (no injection defence, no read-only mode, no approval gate ⇒ fails all five clauses), on **asymmetric account risk**, and on **unestablished accountability**. ✅ **BORROW: M1** the write probe (*on failure change TECHNIQUE, not parameters*) · **M2** D34's detector against the vault's own gates · **M3** deference-as-success · **M4** the `help()` pattern (generate the reference from the code) · **M5** the injection paragraph they never wrote, into the candidate-LLM ADR.

**Workflow:** 14 agents (5 map → 5 pipelined **contradiction** → 3 situate → 1 critic); **11/12 completed, 1 errored, 3 empty-result, ~1.53M subagent tokens, 393 tool uses, 817 s.** ⚠️ 3 contradiction passes + the prior-art agent failed schema ⇒ prior art researched **by hand**; mapper outputs **recovered from `journal.jsonl`**. ⭐⭐ **METHOD — v246's finding REPLICATES and the fix WORKS:** the verify stage was rebuilt as a **contradiction** stage (mapper report handed over as a *suspect document*; job = produce corrections by independent reading; ≥6 citations opened and confirmed; a **read receipt** naming files and line ranges required) and it returned corrections and receipts instead of confirmations. ⇒ **Do not ask an agent whether a claim is true; ask it what the claim gets wrong, and make it show you what it read.** **ERROR LEDGER 9 caught, 5 MINE (all self-caught pre-publication; three were suspicions that did NOT survive checking), 4 the fleet's** — incl. ⭐ the critic "correcting" 148 files to **173** by counting `.git/` internals (**the vault's signature error shape committed by the agent assigned to hunt it** ⇒ *adjudicate a counting dispute with a command, not with authority*) and ⭐ the critic calling the binary **`libneedle.*`** — **v246's** binary, imported from the vault context I supplied ⇒ **rejected**, and *the lesson is mine: context supplied to ground an agent can also contaminate it.*
